Let's design local optimizer in more detail.

Objective with normalized a (sum=1): f(a)=max_k c_k (score=2n f). Minimize f.
Parameter v (logit), a=softmax(v). We want nonnegative smooth.

Soft surrogate: q(v,τ)=logsumexp(c/τ) or weighted sum with weights from soft-max. Gradient of q = (1/τ) ∑ w c/dv? Actually if minimize φ=log∑ exp(c/τ), gradient wrt a = (1/τ)∑ w_k g_k where w=e^{c/τ}/sum. Then wrt v = a*(grad_a - dot(grad_a,a)). Similar to weighted sum but weights scale 1/τ; Adam step size can adapt. Using logsumexp ensures focus on max but smooth. τ can be in units of f (e.g., 0.05 to 0.005 * f). Previous uses weights softmax((b-bmax)/tau) not logsumexp, but equivalent.

Top emphasis: Could use weights w=exp((c-cmax)/τ), then add mass to top m? We can start with pure top-m uniform to stabilize? For max, as τ→0, weights concentrate on exact max lags. But if multiple, uniform over active.

We can implement:
- Compute c = FFT self, length L.
- Determine active count m: maybe max(5, int(0.02 L)) to 0.1 L? Previous top_k 5%. Use mixture: w_soft = exp((c - cmax)/τ). w_top: indicator top-K with uniform. w = α w_soft + (1-α) w_top / K? Or add 0.5 boost to top as previous. We can tune α/tau.
- Gradient wrt a: g = 2 correlation(w,a). Compute via FFT: P=fft(w,m), A=ift(P*conj(F)).real[:n], gradient=2A.
- Since v parameter: grad_v = a*(g - dot(g,a)). Normalize by RMS. Adam step.
- Optional line search: After step, compute score, accept if improves or occasionally if not (simulated annealing). But Adam with softmax and fixed small step okay. We can perform numerical backtracking: try step size lr (0.05-0.2). Since objective non-smooth, use best update.

def score_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S...
    m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=fft(a,m); c=ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]; c=max(c,0)
    bmax=c.max()
    return 2*n*bmax/(S*S), c, m, F
```
But local needs normalized sum1 for objective; we can normalize inside. If a not normalized, scale invariant. Use normalized a for local.

def grad_weights(w, a, m, F):
   P=fft(w, m)
   A=ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
   return 2*A

Pseudo:
```
def local_adam(a, max_seconds, seed, tau_start=0.08, tau_end=0.004, top_frac_start=0.10, top_frac_end=0.02, mix_start=0.2, mix_end=0.7, momentum_reset_samples...):
   rng=...
   v=log(a+1e-300); v-=mean; clip=-25,25
   m=next_pow2(2n-1); L=2n-1
   a=softmax(v); best=score_fft(a); best_a=...
   momentum zero
   t=0
   lr_base=0.12
   no_improve_time=0
   start=now
   while now<deadline:
      # maybe every few iterations recompute score exact? we have c from gradient.
      F=fft(a,m); c=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; c=max(c,0); bmax=c.max()
      score=2n*bmax (since sum1)
      if score<best: update best, last_improve
      tau = bmax*(tau_start*(1-p)+tau_end*p)
      # if tau too small relative? 
      x=(c-bmax)/tau; x-=x.max(); w=exp(x); w/=sum
      topK=max(1,int(L*(top_frac_start*(1-p)+top_frac_end*p)))
      idx=argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
      w_top=zeros; w_top[idx]=1/topK
      mix = mix_start*(1-p)+mix_end*p
      w = mix*w + (1-mix)*w_top
      grad_a=2*corr(w,a,m,F)
      grad_v=a*(grad_a - dot(grad_a,a))
      if norm small: perturb and continue
      Adam update; lr = lr_base*(0.6*p+0.4?) maybe adaptive
      v_new = v - lr*step; mean; clip
      a_new=softmax(v_new)
      s_new=score(a_new) # maybe from c? use exact FFT
      if finite and (s_new < best*0.99999 or s_new < current*0.99): accept
      else:
          # try line search smaller
          for factor in 0.5,0.25:
             v_trial = v - lr*factor*step; ... accept if improve
          if not accept: maybe keep v but reduce lr? if too many rejected, add noise or revert to best.
      t++
      if time-no_improve>limit: reset v=best_a log + noise, momentum=0, last_improve=now
   return best_a, best_score
```

def make_power(n,p,q):
    i=np.arange(n)+1.0
    r=np.arange(n)
    raw = np.exp(-p*np.log(i) + q*np.log(n - r +1.0)) # for q can negative; ensure positive
    return proj_nonneg_sum(raw)

```
def flatten_target(c, mode='clip', strength=1.0):
   L=len(c); c=c.copy(); c=np.maximum(c,0); s=c.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/L
   c/=s; avg=1/L
   if mode='power': t=c**strength
   elif mode='recip': t=1/(1+strength*c/avg)
   elif mode='clip': cap=avg*(1+strength*2.0); t=np.minimum(c, cap); # distribute residual to below cap
       residual=s - t.sum(); low_mask=t<cap-1e-15; if any: t[low_mask]+=residual/low_mask.sum(); else t[:] +=residual/L
   normalize
   return t
```

def spectral_project(a, target, iters=30, blend=0.5, m=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if m is None: m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    target = np.asarray(target,float); if len>L pad/trunc.
    target = np.maximum(target,0); ts=target.sum(); if ts>0 target/=ts
    T=np.fft.fft(target,m)
    a=proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft(a)[0]
    scale=...
    for it in range(iters):
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
       # regularize based on T
       maxT=max(abs(T).max(),1e-12)
       eps=1e-8*maxT
       denom=F + eps
       # Avoid divide by zero where denom small
       Fnew = 0.5*(F + T/denom)
       anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
       pos=np.maximum(anew,0)
       ps=pos.sum()
       if ps <= 1e-12*max(a.sum(),1e-12): pos=a.copy()
       bld=blend*(1 - 0.5*it/iters) + 0.2? # ensure at least .2
       a_new=(1-bld)*a + bld*pos
       a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
       a_new=proj_nonneg_sum(a_new)
       sc=score_fft(a_new)[0]
       if sc<best_sc: best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc
       a=a_new
    return best,best_sc
```

Pseudo detailed local_adam:
```
def local_adam(a0, max_seconds, seed, tau_start=0.08, tau_end=0.004, top_start=0.10, top_end=0.01, mix_start=0.25, mix_end=0.75, lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.04, stuck_sec=45.0):
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; m=next_pow2(L)
    v=log_plus(a0); v-=mean; v=clip(v,-25,25)
    vbest=v.copy(); a=softmax(v); best=score_fft(a)[0]
    mu=zeros; nu=zeros; t=0; lr=lr_start
    start=now; last=now; n_reject=0
    while now<deadline:
        elapsed...
        p=...
        # if long stuck: v = vbest + small noise; mu=nu=0; lr=max(lr,0.08); last=now; n_reject=0
        F=fft(a,m); c=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0); bmax=c.max(); sc=2*n*bmax
        if sc<best: vbest=v.copy(); a_best=a.copy(); best=sc; last=now; n_reject=0; lr=lr_start*(0.5*p+0.5?) maybe increase? 
        tau = max(1e-12, bmax*(tau_start*(1-p)+tau_end*p))
        # If bmax zero break
        x=(c-bmax)/tau; x-=x.max(); w=exp(x)*?; wsum
        topK=...
        idx=argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
        wtop=zeros; wtop[idx]=1.0/topK
        mix=...
        w = mix*w + (1-mix)*wtop; w/=w.sum()
        # grad
        P=fft(w,m); A=ifft(P*conj(F)).real[:n]; grad_a=2*A
        gv=a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,a))
        grms=sqrt(mean(gv*gv)); if small: v+=noise; continue
        gv/=grms
        t+=1; mu=...; nu=...; step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8); step=clip(step,-0.5,0.5)
        lr = lr_start*(1-p)+lr_end*p
        v_new = v - lr*step
        v_new -= mean; clip
        # line search factors [1.0,0.5,0.25]
        accepted=False
        for fac in [1.0,0.5,0.25]:
             vv=v - lr*fac*step; normalize; aa=softmax(vv); F=fft; c; sc=...
             if finite and sc >= best*0.999? Accept if sc < current score? We need current score variable maybe sc_current.
        choose best trial; if best_trial_score < sc_current or (best_trial_score < best and fac< some) accept: v=trial; a=trial_a; sc_current=trial_score; update momentum? If accepted, okay.
        else: n_reject++; v unchanged; maybe reduce lr *=0.8; if n_reject>20 reset to best + noise.
    final score; return a_best,best
```
Current score variable update. The line search tries multiple FFTs (3). Could be expensive. Maybe just one trial and if worse > tol reset. But line search improves.

`score_fft_norm(a, m=None)` expects sum1 and returns score, c, m.
```
def score_fft_norm(a, m=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf, empty, 0
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: ...
    a=a/s
    if m is None: m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    # numerical small negative
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    sc=float(2.0*n*c.max())
    return sc,c,m
```
For nonnormalized, score invariant; we can normalize to sum1. For evaluate_sequence exact uses original values but scale invariant if positive? It clips to [0,1000]; if we normalize sum1, fine.

`score_raw(a)` maybe if not normalized. But normalize.

`interp_seq(a,n)`:
```
def interp_seq(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return ones/n
    a=a/s
    if a.size==n: return a
    x=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); y=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(y,x,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<=0: fill ones/n; else b/=s
    return b
```
Interpolating a singular endpoint: Using linear interp across n points. Good.

`make_power(n,p,q)`:
```
def make_power(n,p,q):
    n=int(max(3,n)); i=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float); r=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    loga=-p*np.log(i)+q*np.log(n-r)
    loga-=np.max(loga) (avoid overflow)
    return proj_nonneg_sum(np.exp(loga))
```

For p<0, sequence increasing? Convolution maybe reversed same as reverse; including p<0 maybe useful (mass right). But reverse of p>0 is equivalent after reversal; score same. However right factor q combined breaks reversal; p<0,q equivalent reverse of p> -q. Since score invariant under reversal of whole sequence? If a reversed, convolution reversed max same. def corr_weights(w, a, m, F):
    P=np.fft.fft(w, m)
    A=np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:len(a)]
    return A

Let's add a `perturb_v` function:
```
def perturb_v(v, rng, sigma, n):
    v = v + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
    v -= v.mean(); v = np.clip(v,-30,30)
    return v
```

A "nudge active pairs" heuristic: For current a, top lag k. Gradient descent already. Could add:

Let's write local_adam carefully.

```
def local_adam(a0, max_seconds, seed, tau_start=0.10, tau_end=0.003, top_start=0.12, top_end=0.02, mix_start=0.20, mix_end=0.80, lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.05, stuck_sec=50.0):
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    rng=...
    v=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); v-=v.mean(); v=np.clip(v,-25,25)
    a=softmax(v)
    best_sc, _, _ = score_fft_norm(a,m); vbest=v.copy(); abest=a.copy()
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=lr_start; start=time.monotonic(); last_improve=start; reject_count=0; sc_cur=best_sc
    deadline=start+max_seconds
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        now=time.monotonic(); p=min(1.0,(now-start)/max_seconds)
        if now - last_improve > stuck_sec:
            # restart from best with noise
            v = vbest.copy()
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.15*(1-p)
            v += rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n)
            v = normalize_v(v)
            mu.fill(0); nu.fill(0); t=0; lr=max(lr, lr_start*0.7); reject_count=0; last_improve=now; _=now
            # maybe also try spectral? No, inside just noise
        # F and c
        F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
        c=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
        bmax=float(c.max())
        if not finite or <=1e-300: break
        sc_cur=2.0*n*bmax
        if sc_cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc_cur; abest=a.copy(); vbest=v.copy(); last_improve=now; reject_count=0
            # increase lr slightly?
            lr = min(0.18, lr*1.02)
        if bmax <= 1e-300: break
        tau = bmax * (tau_start*(1-p) + tau_end*p)
        # if tau too small relative to finite precision
        if tau <= 1e-12*bmax: tau=1e-6*bmax
        x=(c - bmax)/tau; x = x - np.max(x)
        # clip x to avoid overflow
        x = np.clip(x,-700,0)
        w=np.exp(x); ws=w.sum()
        if not finite or ws<=0: w=np.ones(L)/L
        else: w/=ws
        top_frac = top_start*(1-p)+top_end*p
        topK=int(L*top_frac); topK=max(8,min(L,topK))
        # for large L topK maybe 200; okay
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
        wtop=np.zeros(L); wtop[idx]=1.0/topK
        mix = mix_start*(1-p)+mix_end*p
        w = mix*w + (1.0-mix)*wtop
        ws=w.sum(); if ws>0: w/=ws
        # gradient
        P=np.fft.fft(w,m)
        A=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        grad_a=2.0*A
        gv=a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,a))
        grms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv*gv)))
        if not finite or grms<1e-16:
            v=perturb_v(vbest,rng,0.1,n); mu=nu=0; a=softmax(v); continue
        gv /= grms
        t +=1
        beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
        mu = beta1*mu + (1-beta1)*gv
        nu = beta2*nu + (1-beta2)*(gv*gv)
        b1=beta1**t; b2=beta2**t
        mhat=mu/max(1e-12,1-b1)
        vhat=nu/max(1e-12,1-b2)
        step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        step=np.clip(step,-0.75,0.75)
        lr_cur = lr_start*(1-p)+lr_end*p
        v_new = v - lr_cur*step
        v_new = normalize_v(v_new)
        a_new=softmax(v_new)
        Fnew=np.fft.fft(a_new,m)
        cnew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew*Fnew).real[:L]; cnew=np.maximum(cnew,0)
        sc_new=float(2.0*n*cnew.max())
        if not finite: sc_new=np.inf
        # accept? if sc_new < sc_cur or sc_new < best_sc or sc_new < sc_cur*1.05
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < min(sc_cur, best_sc) * 1.08:
            v=v_new; a=a_new; sc_cur=sc_new; reject_count=0
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc_new; abest=a.copy(); vbest=v.copy(); last_improve=now
        else:
            reject_count +=1
            # try smaller factor maybe
            if reject_count % 3 ==0 and lr_cur>0.02:
                for fac in (0.5,0.25):
                    v_try = normalize_v(v - lr_cur*fac*step)
                    a_try=softmax(v_try)
                    Ftry=fft; ctry=...
                    sc_try=...
                    if finite and sc_try < sc_cur:
                         v=v_try; a=a_try; sc_cur=sc_try; reject_count=0
                         break
            if reject_count > 30:
                v=normalize_v(vbest + rng.normal(0,0.08,n)); a=softmax(v); sc_cur=best_sc; mu=nu=0; t=0; lr*=0.7; reject_count=0
    # final
    sc_final=score_fft_norm(a,m)[0]; if < best... 
    return abest,best_sc
```
`normalize_v` = mean subtract and clip. Use top-level.

Potential performance: Every iteration computes score c; then gradient uses F same. If accept, we computed cnew for new a. That's one extra FFT and ifft per iteration. If we don't check, update may diverge. We can trust small step and periodically check. Simpler: update v/a, then next loop score. If score becomes inf, reset. To avoid bad states, if step too large maybe reject. But line search one score doubles cost; still okay? For n=8192, each iteration 3 FFTs (F, P, ifft cnew) plus gradients, maybe 4. 1000 iters heavy. We can check every few iterations:
- After computing v_new/a_new, don't compute score immediately; set a=a_new, v=v_new, sc_cur=None. At next loop, compute F/c/sc and if >best*2 reset. This halves cost. We'll implement without line search score. If `sc_cur` after compute > best*1.5, revert to best and reset. This is robust.

```
a_current updated; at top compute sc_cur; if sc_cur > best_sc*1.5 and not first: revert; continue
```

def softmax(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    v-=v.max()

    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return ones/n

```
def make_rand_logpoly(n, rng, kmax=16, amp=1.5):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n); v=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,kmax+1):
      amp_k=amp/(k**0.65)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      v+=amp_k*np.cos(2π*k*t+ph)
   v-=v.mean(); v=np.clip(v,-6,6); return softmax(v)
```
This may produce bumpy shapes. Could be too noisy. Include few.

`flatten_target(c, mode, strength)`:
```
L=len(c); c=np.maximum(c,0); s=c.sum(); if s<=0 return ones/L; c/=s; avg=1/L
if mode=='power': t=np.power(c, strength)
elif mode=='recip': t=1.0/(1.0 + strength*(c/avg))
elif mode=='clip': cap=avg*(1.0+2.0*strength); t=np.minimum(c, cap); resid=float(c.sum()-t.sum()); mask=t < cap-1e-12; if mask.any(): t[mask] += resid/mask.sum(); elif resid>0: t=t+resid/L
elif mode='power_recip': combine? skip
t=np.maximum(t,0); ts=t.sum(); if ts>0 t/=ts else ones
```
For `recip`, sum not 1; normalize. It heavily raises lows. Good.

Potential alternative: Use the SOTA LP idea with scipy linprog. Could improve significantly if we implement optional for n up to 1024. Let's consider adding a safe LP-based directional update.

LP: For current normalized a and current M=max c. We want b≥0 maximize sum b subject to C b ≤ M, where C_{k,i}=a_{k-i} if valid (with 2? c_k(a,b)=∑ a_i b_{k-i}+a_{k-i}b_i? Wait bilinear not symmetric? If updating f -> g with constraint ||f*g||∞≤M. The convolution of a and b full: d_k=∑ a_i b_{k-i}. This linear in b. Its derivative? The SOTA LP used f*g, not a*a. For fixed a, maximize sum b s.t. conv(a,b)≤M. If b normalized sum1, and update a=(1-t)a+t b, maybe M decreases. This direction b is different from gradient of max a*a: It maximizes sum of b while keeping cross-convolution with a under M, rather than reducing weighted sum of a*a. Could be better.

Can we approximate b without full LP using a simple formula? The constraints: for each k, ∑_i C_{k,i} b_i ≤ M. C is convolution of a with kernel. This is a linear packing. The optimal b might put mass where dual constraints dual variables low. The LP dual: minimize M∑_k y_k subject to ∑_k C_{k,i} y_k ≥1, y_k≥0. Here C_{k,i}=a_{k-i} if valid (no factor 2). The dual constraint for i: ∑_k a_{k-i} y_k ≥1. This is same correlation A_i as gradient. If we can find y, then complementary slackness: b_i>0 where ∑ a_{k-i} y_k =1. We can find y via multiplicative weights: start y uniform over active? Minimize M∑y with constraints. This is a convex optimization (dual) linear program. Could solve with projected gradient? Maybe simpler:
   Initialize y_k ∝ exp(-η violation?) Actually constraints A_i(y)≥1. Use Fenchel? 
   Multiplicative weight for packing: Primal max 1^T b s.t. C b ≤ M, b≥0. Dual min M 1^T y s.t. C^T y ≥1. We can run multiplicative weights for b directly:
      b_i ∝ 1 / (1 + μ * (C? no) ). Standard MW: b_i = exp(-μ ∑_k c_{ki} y_k?) where y updated by constraint violations. Might approximate.
   Then b = primal iterates.
But implementing robust in time is too much.

C=np.zeros((L,n))
for k in range(L):

```

`score_fft_norm` returns (score, c, m). We may not need c for score only, but local uses. Provide:
```
def score_fft_norm(a, m=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf, empty, 0
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a,0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=f; if s<=0: return inf, empty, 0
    a=a/s
    if m is None: m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    sc=float(2.0*n*c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return inf,c,m
    return sc,c,m
```

`make_power_three(n,p,q,tilt)`:
```
def make_power_three(n,p,q,tilt=0.0):
    n=int(max(3,n))
    i=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    r=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    if n>1: t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    else: t=np.zeros(1)
    left=-p*np.log(i)
    right=q*np.log(n-r)  # n-r in 1..n
    lin=tilt*t
    loga=left+right+lin
    mx=float(np.max(loga))
    if not finite: loga.fill(0)
    else: loga=loga-mx
    return proj_nonneg_sum(np.exp(loga))
```
Check p can be negative? log term fine. q negative with n-r min1. tilt log? tilt*t linear in v.

`make_rand_logpoly(n,rng,kmax=14,amp=1.2)`:
```
t=np.linspace(0,1,n); v=zeros
for k in range(1,kmax+1):
   amp_k=amp/(k**0.65)
   ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   v += amp_k*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t+ph)
v-=v.mean(); v=np.clip(v,-5,5)
return softmax(v)
```
Could also include sin with random phase already.

`add_candidate` top-level:
```
def add_candidate(cands, seen, a, div=0.05, max_seen=60):
    if a is None or len(a)==0: return
    if len(cands) >= max_seen: return
    a=proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    sc=score_fft_norm(a)[0]
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    for b in seen:
       # if b length differs? interp before add. Ensure same length.
       if b.size != a.size: continue
       d=float(np.sqrt(np.sum((a-b)*(a-b))))
       if d < div: return
    seen.append(a.copy())
    cands.append((sc, a.copy()))
    cands.sort(key=pair_score)
```
No removal. max_seen 80.

`spectral_project(...)`: Need `score_fft_norm` may be costly if each iter. Score every 5 and start/end.
```
def spectral_project(a, target, iters=30, blend=0.55, m=None, score_interval=5):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if target is None: return proj_nonneg_sum(a), score_fft_norm(a)[0]
   if m is None: m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   a=proj_nonneg_sum(a)
   t=np.asarray(target,float); if len(t)<L pad else trunc; nonneg; s=t.sum(); if s<=0 return a, score
   t=t/s
   T=np.fft.fft(t,m)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft_norm(a,m)[0]
   maxTmax=... maybe 1.0
   for it in range(iters):
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
       if not all finite break
       maxT=float(np.max(np.abs(T))) if T.size else 1.0; if <=0 maxT=1.0
       eps=1e-7*maxT + 1e-12
       denom=F + eps
       with np.errstate(...):
          Fnew=0.5*(F + T/denom)
       Fnew=np.nan_to_num(Fnew, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
       anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
       mx=float(np.max(np.abs(anew))); if mx>1e6: anew/=mx
       pos=np.maximum(anew,0)
       ps=pos.sum()
       if ps <= 1e-12: pos=a
       bld = blend * (1.0 - 0.5*it/max(1,iters-1))
       bld = max(0.22, min(0.9, bld))
       a_new=(1-bld)*a + bld*pos
       a_new=proj_nonneg_sum(a_new)
       if (it+1) % score_interval ==0 or it==iters-1:
          sc=score_fft_norm(a_new,m)[0]
          if finite and sc<best_sc: best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc
       a=a_new
   return best,best_sc
```
Potential: If target sum1, but a sum1, first Fnew maybe? Good.

`local_adam` code in detail. Need not use nested. Let's write robust.

```
def local_adam(a0, max_seconds, seed, tau_start=0.10, tau_end=0.004, top_start=0.10, top_end=0.02, mix_start=0.25, mix_end=0.80, lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.05):
    a0=proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
    n=len(a0); L=2*n-1; m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    v=log_plus(a0); v=normalize_v(v)
    a=softmax(v)
    best_sc, _, _ = score_fft_norm(a,m)
    vbest=v.copy(); abest=a.copy()
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
    stuck_limit=max(15.0, min(120.0, 0.30*max_seconds))
    last_improve=start
    iter_no=0
    lr=lr_start
    divergence_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            v = vbest.copy()
            sigma = 0.10 + 0.20*((time.monotonic()-start)/max_seconds if max_seconds>0 else 0.0)
            v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v=normalize_v(v)
            a=softmax(v)
            mu.fill(0.0); nu.fill(0.0); t=0; divergence_count=0; last_improve=time.monotonic()
            continue
        F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
        c=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
        c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        bmax=float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        sc=2.0*n*bmax
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc > best_sc * 1.6 and iter_no > 10:
            a=abest.copy(); v=vbest.copy(); mu.fill(0); nu.fill(0); t=0; last_improve=time.monotonic(); divergence_count+=1
            if divergence_count>6: # maybe reset to pure best no noise
                break? No, continue with noise?
            continue
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; abest=a.copy(); vbest=v.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
            divergence_count=0
            lr=min(0.18, lr*1.01)
        p=float((time.monotonic()-start)/max_seconds) if max_seconds>1e-6 else 1.0
        if p>1: p=1
        tau=bmax*(tau_start*(1.0-p)+tau_end*p)
        if tau <= 1e-6*bmax: tau=1e-6*bmax
        x=(c-bmax)/tau
        x=np.clip(x,-700.0,0.0)
        w=np.exp(x)
        ws=float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws<=0:
            w=np.ones(L,dtype=float)/float(L)
        else:
            w=w/ws
        top_frac=top_start*(1.0-p)+top_end*p
        topK=int(L*top_frac)
        if topK<8: topK=8
        if topK>L: topK=L
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
        wtop=np.zeros(L,dtype=float)
        wtop[idx]=1.0/float(topK)
        mix=mix_start*(1.0-p)+mix_end*p
        w=mix*w + (1.0-mix)*wtop
        ws=float(w.sum())
        if ws>0 and np.isfinite(ws): w=w/ws
        P=np.fft.fft(w,m)
        A=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        grad_a=2.0*A
        gv=a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,a))
        grms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv*gv)))
        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-16:
            v=normalize_v(vbest + rng.normal(0.0,0.08,n))
            a=softmax(v); mu.fill(0); nu.fill(0); t=0; last_improve=time.monotonic(); continue
        gv=gv/grms
        t +=1
        b1=0.9; b2=0.999
        mu=b1*mu + (1.0-b1)*gv
        nu=b2*nu + (1.0-b2)*(gv*gv)
        b1t=b1**t; b2t=b2**t
        mhat=mu/max(1e-12,1.0-b1t)
        vhat=nu/max(1e-12,1.0-b2t)
        step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        step=np.clip(step,-0.8,0.8)
        lr_cur=lr_start*(1.0-p)+lr_end*p
        v_new=v - lr_cur*step
        v_new=normalize_v(v_new)
        a_new=softmax(v_new)
        # sanity if a_new has nonfinite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
            a=abest.copy(); v=vbest.copy(); mu.fill(0); nu.fill(0); t=0; continue
        v=v_new; a=a_new
        iter_no +=1
    # once final
    sc,_,_=score_fft_norm(a,m)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; abest=a.copy()
    return abest, float(best_sc)
```
No line search score. If update diverges, reset. Could be too aggressive; step size lr 0.12, gradient normalized, v changes finite. Softmax okay. Diversion check after update next loop; if diverged reset. Good.

Potential issue: `np.argpartition(c, -topK)` if topK==L? Error? If topK>=L, use all. Add:

if topK>=L: idx=np.arange(L) else: arg
```

```
def run_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, rng):
    n=int(max(3,n)); now=time.monotonic()
    if now >= stage_deadline - 5:
       if best_a is not None: sc=score_fft_norm(interp_seq(best_a,n))[0]; return interp_seq(best_a,n), sc
       return None, inf
    cands=[]; seen=[]
    # add best global for n
    if best_a is not None:
       add_candidate(cands, seen, interp_seq(best_a,n))
    # power scan
    plist=[0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.55,0.65,0.8]
    qlist=[-0.25,-0.1,0.0,0.1,0.25,0.4]
    for p in plist:
       for q in qlist:
          if len(cands)>=80: break
          add_candidate(cands, seen, make_power_three(n,p,q,0.0))
       if len(cands)>=80: break
    # tilt
    for p in [0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65]:
       for rr in [-0.8,-0.4,0.4,0.8]:
          add_candidate(...)
    # random logpoly 6
    for _ in range(6):
       add_candidate(cands, seen, make_rand_logpoly(n,rng,kmax=12,amp=1.2))
    # spectral if enough time and n<=4096
    rem=stage_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0 and n <= 4096:
       spec=spectral_candidates(n, best_a if best_a is not None else None, min(stage_deadline-20, time.monotonic()+min(rem*0.35,180.0)), rng, max_runs=4)
       for a in spec: add_candidate(cands, seen, a)
    # sort cands
    cands.sort(key=pair_score)
    if not cands:
       a=np.ones(n)/n; sc=score_fft_norm(a)[0]; return a,sc
    stage_best=a; stage_score=best global? Actually initialize to best global interpolated score from cands[0]
    stage_best_a, stage_best_sc = cands[0]
    # local top 3
    top=cands[:4]
    weights=[0.5,0.25,0.15,0.1]
    for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(top):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now + 15 >= stage_deadline: break
       rem=stage_deadline - now - 5.0
       if rem <= 0: break
       w=weights[idx] if idx < len(weights) else 0.1
       alloc=max(15.0, min(900.0, w*rem))
       # If current global best score for this length better? start from candidate anyway.
       a_opt, sc_opt = local_adam(a, alloc, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1)))
       if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < stage_best_sc:
           stage_best_a=a_opt; stage_best_sc=sc_opt
       print
    # final local on stage best if time
    if time.monotonic() + 10 < stage_deadline:
       rem=stage_deadline-time.monotonic()-5.0
       if rem > 30.0:
          a_opt, sc_opt=local_adam(stage_best_a, rem, seed=...)
          if sc_opt<stage_best_sc: update
    return stage_best_a, stage_best_sc
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed=int(seed); budget_s=float(max(60,budget_s))
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(60,budget_s-30)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    prev=load_previous()
    best_a=prev
    best_score=score_fft_norm(prev)[0] if prev is not None else inf
    print
    # Decide stages
    stages=[]
    base_n = len(prev) if prev is not None else 2048
    # make powers of two around base: 512? 1024? 2048,4096,8192,16384
    candidates_n=[1024,2048,4096,8192,16384]
    # filter: include if base_n>=1024? maybe include 512 if small.
    order=[]
    for n in candidates_n:
       order.append(n)
    # remove duplicates. Add base_n if not in.
    # We want increasing. If prev length 2048, include 1024 then 2048 etc.
    # Allocate fractions based on position maybe.
    # Ensure last larger. Maybe fractions [0.05,0.10,0.20,0.35,0.30]?
    # But if base_n=2048, more at 2048/4096/8192.
    fracs=[0.05,0.12,0.22,0.30,0.31]
    # If time small, skip large.
    for i,n in enumerate(order):
       if n < 512: continue
       if n > 16384: continue
       frac=fracs[min(i,len(fracs)-1)]
       stages.append((n,frac))
    # But if base_n=2048 and we include 1024 first, okay.
    # We should probably not spend too much on 1024. Good.
    # Process stages until deadline
    for n,frac in stages:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now+30 >= deadline: break
       stage_time=(deadline-start)*frac
       # maybe ensure 20s min
       stage_deadline=min(deadline-5, now+max(20,stage_time))
       print stage
       a_new, sc_new=run_stage(int(n), stage_deadline, best_a, rng)
       if a_new is not None and finite and sc_new < best_score -1e-9:
          best_a=a_new; best_score=sc_new
          print
       # if no improvement for larger, still maybe keep best shorter.
    # final local at best length
    if best_a is None: best_a=np.ones(512)/512; best_score=score_fft_norm(best_a)[0]
    if time.monotonic()+20 < deadline:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()-10
       if rem>30:
          a_opt, sc_opt=local_adam(best_a, rem, seed=int(rng.integers(...)))
          if finite and sc_opt<best_score: best_a=a_opt; best_score=sc_opt
    # maybe interpolate to next power if time and improve? Could try one larger length by interpolation + short local.
    if time.monotonic()+120 < deadline:
       next_n = min(16384, max(512, 2*len(best_a)))
       a_try=interp_seq(best_a,next_n)
       sc_try=score_fft_norm(a_try)[0]
       if finite and sc_try < best_score + 0.05: # worth
          a_opt, sc_opt=local_adam(a_try, max(30, deadline-time.monotonic()-30), seed=...)
          if sc_opt<best_score: update
    # validate exact
    best_a=proj_nonneg_sum(best_a)
    if len(best_a) <= 20000:
       try: exact=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a]); print; if not finite fallback
       except: pass
    else: maybe no exact
    # Ensure values nonnegative and finite
    out=[float(max(0.0,x)) for x in best_a]
    # Optional evaluate_sequence clips to 1000, values tiny.
    return out
```

def load_previous():
    try:
        cand = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    except Exception:
        cand = None
    if isinstance(cand, list): arr=np.asarray(cand,float)
    else: ...
    if arr.ndim==0: arr=arr.reshape(1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if arr.size>0 and np.isfinite(s) and s>0:
       return arr/s
    return None
```

`normalize_v`:
```
def normalize_v(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
       v=np.zeros_like(v)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
    return v
```

def make_bump(n,p,q,c,amp,w):

Top-level:
```
def make_scaled_bump(a, center, width, amp):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   factor=1.0+amp*np.exp(-(((t-center)/width)**2))
   return proj_nonneg_sum(a*np.maximum(factor,0.0))
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
  - FFT-based exact score and exact gradient of a softmax/soft-max surrogate.
  - diverse constructive starts: interpolated previous best, one/two-exponent
    power laws, logarithmic tilts, smooth random Fourier shapes, Gaussian
    bumps.
  - spectral square-root projections onto flattened / shaped convolution
    targets.
  - Adam-like optimization in log-softmax space with annealed soft peaks and
    top-lag emphasis.  Multiple stages over increasing lengths are run, and the
    best sequence found is returned.
"""
import numpy as np
import time


def pair_score(x):
    return x[0]


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def proj_nonneg_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    return a


def normalize_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
    return v


def softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(v, -745.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(v.shape, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    return e / s


def log_plus(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    return np.log(a)


def score_fft_norm(a, m=None):
    """Score normalized to sum 1. Returns (score, convolution, m)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), 0
    a = a / s
    if m is None:
        m = next_pow2(max(1, 2 * n - 1))
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = float("inf")
    return sc, c, m


def score_seq(a):
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = arr / s
    return score_fft_norm(arr)[0]


def interp_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bs = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(bs) or bs <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return b / bs


def make_power_three(n, p=0.5, q=0.0, tilt=0.0):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    r = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    loga = -p * np.log(i) + q * np.log(n - r) + tilt * t
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
        return proj_nonneg_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
    return proj_nonneg_sum(np.exp(np.clip(loga, -700.0, 0.0)))


def make_rand_logpoly(n, rng, kmax=12, amp=1.2):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, int(kmax) + 1):
        ak = amp / (float(k) ** 0.70)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        v += ak * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
    v = normalize_v(v)
    v = np.clip(v, -5.0, 5.0)
    return softmax(v)


def make_scaled_bump(a, center=0.5, width=0.15, amp=0.4):
    a = proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    w = max(1e-3, float(width))
    factor = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.exp(-(((t - float(center)) / w) ** 2))
    factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
    return proj_nonneg_sum(a * factor)


def normalize_target(t):
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 0.0)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    s = float(t.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return t / s


def make_edge_target(L, ew=0.0, ep=2.0):
    L = int(max(3, L))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    edge = np.abs(x - 0.5) * 2.0
    t = 1.0 + float(ew) * (edge ** float(ep))
    return normalize_target(t)


def flatten_target(c, mode="recip", strength=0.8):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(1, L), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)
    c = c / s
    avg = 1.0 / float(L)

    if mode == "power":
        t = np.power(c, max(0.05, float(strength)))
    elif mode == "clip":
        cap = avg * (1.0 + 2.0 * float(strength))
        t = np.minimum(c, cap)
        resid = float(c.sum() - t.sum())
        if resid > 0.0:
            mask = t < (cap - 1e-12)
            if np.any(mask):
                t[mask] += resid / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            else:
                t = t + resid / float(L)
    else:  # recip
        t = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(1e-6, float(strength)) * (c / avg))

    return normalize_target(t)


def spectral_project(a, target, iters=40, blend=0.60, m=None, score_interval=5):
    a = proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if m is None:
        m = next_pow2(max(1, L))

    tg = normalize_target(target)
    if tg is None:
        sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
        return a, sc
    tg = np.asarray(tg, dtype=np.float64)
    if tg.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:tg.size] = tg
        tg = tmp
    else:
        tg = tg[:L].copy()
    tg = normalize_target(tg)
    if tg is None:
        sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
        return a, sc

    T = np.fft.fft(tg, m)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_fft_norm(a, m)[0]
    max_abs_T = float(np.max(np.abs(T))) if T.size else 1.0
    if not np.isfinite(max_abs_T) or max_abs_T <= 0.0:
        max_abs_T = 1.0

    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    for it in range(iters):
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
            break
        eps = 1e-7 * max_abs_T + 1e-12
        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            Fnew = 0.5 * (F + T / (F + eps))
        Fnew = np.nan_to_num(Fnew, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
        anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(anew)))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx > 1e6:
            if np.isfinite(mx) and mx > 0.0:
                anew = anew / mx
            else:
                anew = a.copy()
        pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-12:
            pos = a.copy()

        bld = float(blend) * (1.0 - 0.50 * it / float(max(1, iters - 1)))
        bld = max(0.22, min(0.90, bld))
        a_new = (1.0 - bld) * a + bld * pos
        a_new = proj_nonneg_sum(a_new)

        if (it + 1) % int(score_interval) == 0 or it == iters - 1:
            sc = score_fft_norm(a_new, m)[0]
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best = a_new.copy()
                best_sc = sc
        a = a_new

    return best, float(best_sc)


def spectral_candidates(n, base_a, deadline, rng, max_runs=4):
    out = []
    n = int(max(3, n))
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline - 25.0:
        return out

    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = next_pow2(max(1, L))

    starts = []
    if base_a is not None:
        starts.append(proj_nonneg_sum(interp_seq(base_a, n)))
    starts.append(make_power_three(n, 0.50, 0.0, 0.0))
    starts.append(make_power_three(n, 0.45, 0.15, -0.10))
    starts.append(make_power_three(n, 0.55, -0.10, 0.15))

    targets = []
    tg = normalize_target(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))
    if tg is not None:
        targets.append(tg)
    for ew, ep in [(0.30, 2.0), (0.80, 2.0), (1.50, 2.0),
                   (-0.35, 2.0), (-0.75, 2.0), (0.0, 4.0)]:
        tg = make_edge_target(L, ew, ep)
        if tg is not None:
            targets.append(tg)

    if base_a is not None:
        a_seed = proj_nonneg_sum(interp_seq(base_a, n))
        _, c_seed, _ = score_fft_norm(a_seed, m)
        for mode, st in [("recip", 0.7), ("recip", 1.3),
                         ("clip", 0.8), ("power", 0.85)]:
            tg = flatten_target(c_seed, mode, st)
            if tg is not None:
                targets.append(tg)

    used = 0
    for st in starts:
        if used >= int(max_runs):
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        for tg in targets:
            if used >= int(max_runs):
                break
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
                break
            iters = 45 if n <= 2048 else 32
            a, sc = spectral_project(st, tg, iters=iters, blend=0.62, m=m)
            out.append((a, sc))
            used += 1
            break

    res = []
    for a, sc in out:
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            res.append(proj_nonneg_sum(a))
    return res


def add_candidate(cands, seen, a, div=0.05, max_seen=140):
    if a is None:
        return
    if len(cands) >= int(max_seen):
        return
    a = proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    for b in seen:
        if b.size != a.size:
            continue
        d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((a - b) * (a - b))))
        if d < float(div):
            return
    seen.append(a.copy())
    cands.append((sc, a.copy()))
    cands.sort(key=pair_score)


def local_adam(a0, max_seconds, seed,
               tau_start=0.10, tau_end=0.004,
               top_start=0.10, top_end=0.02,
               mix_start=0.25, mix_end=0.80,
               lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.05):
    a0 = proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a0, float("inf")

    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = next_pow2(max(1, L))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    max_seconds = float(max(1e-3, max_seconds))

    v = normalize_v(log_plus(a0))
    a = softmax(v)
    best_sc, _, _ = score_fft_norm(a, m)
    vbest = v.copy()
    abest = a.copy()

    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    stuck_limit = max(10.0, min(120.0, 0.30 * max_seconds))
    last_improve = start
    divergence = 0
    current_sc = float("inf")
    lr = float(lr_start)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            v = vbest.copy()
            prog = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_seconds))
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.18 * (1.0 - prog)
            v = normalize_v(v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            a = softmax(v)
            mu.fill(0.0)
            nu.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_sc = best_sc
            lr = max(0.08, float(lr_start) * 0.85)
            divergence = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            continue

        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        c = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        bmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        sc = float(2.0 * n * bmax)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if np.isfinite(current_sc):
            if sc > current_sc * 1.08:
                lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.72)
            elif sc < current_sc * 0.998:
                lr = min(0.20, lr * 1.04)
        current_sc = sc

        if sc > best_sc * 1.8 and it > 6:
            a = abest.copy()
            v = vbest.copy()
            mu.fill(0.0)
            nu.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_sc = best_sc
            divergence += 1
            if divergence > 12:
                break
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            continue

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            abest = a.copy()
            vbest = v.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            divergence = 0

        prog = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_seconds))
        tau = bmax * (float(tau_start) * (1.0 - prog) + float(tau_end) * prog)
        if tau <= 1e-6 * bmax:
            tau = 1e-6 * bmax

        x = (c - bmax) / tau
        x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(x)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            w = w / ws

        top_frac = float(top_start) * (1.0 - prog) + float(top_end) * prog
        topK = int(L * top_frac)
        if topK < 8:
            topK = 8
        if topK >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
        wtop = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        wtop[idx] = 1.0 / float(topK)

        mixv = float(mix_start) * (1.0 - prog) + float(mix_end) * prog
        w = mixv * w + (1.0 - mixv) * wtop
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if np.isfinite(ws) and ws > 0.0:
            w = w / ws
        else:
            w = wtop

        P = np.fft.fft(w, m)
        A = np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        grad_a = 2.0 * A
        gv = a * (grad_a - float(np.dot(grad_a, a)))
        grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv * gv)))

        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-16:
            v = normalize_v(vbest + rng.normal(0.0, 0.07, n))
            a = softmax(v)
            mu.fill(0.0)
            nu.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_sc = best_sc
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            continue

        gv = gv / grms
        t += 1
        beta1 = 0.90
        beta2 = 0.999
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gv
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gv * gv)

        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -0.80, 0.80)

        lrc = float(lr_start) * (1.0 - prog) + float(lr_end) * prog
        v_new = normalize_v(v - lrc * step)
        a_new = softmax(v_new)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
            a = abest.copy()
            v = vbest.copy()
            mu.fill(0.0)
            nu.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_sc = best_sc
            continue

        v = v_new
        a = a_new
        it += 1

    sc, _, _ = score_fft_norm(a, m)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        abest = a.copy()
    return abest, float(best_sc)


def make_fallback(prev):
    if prev is not None:
        f = proj_nonneg_sum(prev)
        sc = score_fft_norm(f)[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return f
    n = 512
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def load_previous():
    try:
        cand = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    except Exception:
        cand = None
    if cand is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim == 0:
        arr = arr.reshape(1)
    else:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def run_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, rng):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= stage_deadline - 5.0:
        if best_a is not None:
            a = interp_seq(best_a, n)
            sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
            return a, sc
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
        return a, sc

    cands = []
    seen = []

    best_n = None
    if best_a is not None:
        best_n = interp_seq(best_a, n)
        add_candidate(cands, seen, best_n)

        vbase = normalize_v(log_plus(best_n))
        for _ in range(3):
            add_candidate(cands, seen, softmax(vbase + rng.normal(0.0, 0.16, n)))

        for ctr in (0.15, 0.35, 0.55, 0.75, 0.90):
            for amp in (0.25, 0.60):
                add_candidate(cands, seen, make_scaled_bump(best_n, ctr, 0.16, amp))

    p_list = (0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75)
    q_list = (-0.25, 0.0, 0.15, 0.35)
    for p in p_list:
        for q in q_list:
            add_candidate(cands, seen, make_power_three(n, p, q, 0.0))

    for p in (0.40, 0.50, 0.60):
        for rr in (-0.9, -0.4, 0.4, 0.9):
            add_candidate(cands, seen, make_power_three(n, p, 0.0, rr))

    base_p = make_power_three(n, 0.50, 0.0, 0.0)
    for ctr in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75):
        for amp in (0.25, 0.70):
            add_candidate(cands, seen, make_scaled_bump(base_p, ctr, 0.18, amp))

    for _ in range(5):
        add_candidate(cands, seen, make_rand_logpoly(n, rng, kmax=12, amp=1.25))

    rem = stage_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0 and n <= 4096:
        spec_budget = min(rem * 0.35, 180.0)
        spec_deadline = min(stage_deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, spec_budget))
        specs = spectral_candidates(n, best_a, spec_deadline, rng, max_runs=4)
        for a in specs:
            add_candidate(cands, seen, a)

    cands.sort(key=pair_score)
    if not cands:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        sc = score_fft_norm(a)[0]
        return a, sc

    stage_best_a, stage_best_sc = cands[0]

    top = cands[:4]
    weights = (0.52, 0.24, 0.14, 0.10)

    for idx in range(len(top)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 20.0 >= stage_deadline:
            break
        rem = stage_deadline - now - 7.0
        if rem <= 0.0:
            break
        wt = weights[idx] if idx < len(weights) else 0.10
        alloc = max(20.0, min(1200.0, wt * rem))
        a_cand, sc_cand = top[idx]
        a_opt, sc_opt = local_adam(a_cand, alloc, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < stage_best_sc:
            stage_best_a = a_opt
            stage_best_sc = sc_opt
        print("    stage n=%d cand=%d best=%.8f" % (n, idx, stage_best_sc), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() + 25.0 < stage_deadline:
        rem = stage_deadline - time.monotonic() - 7.0
        if rem > 25.0:
            a_opt, sc_opt = local_adam(stage_best_a, rem, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < stage_best_sc:
                stage_best_a = a_opt
                stage_best_sc = sc_opt
            print("    stage n=%d polish best=%.8f" % (n, stage_best_sc), flush=True)

    return stage_best_a, float(stage_best_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    prev = load_previous()
    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    if prev is not None:
        best_a = proj_nonneg_sum(prev)
        best_sc = score_fft_norm(best_a)[0]
    print("Previous score: %.10f" % best_sc, flush=True)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_fallback(None)
        best_sc = score_fft_norm(best_a)[0]

    stage_ns = (1024, 2048, 4096, 8192)
    fracs = (0.05, 0.13, 0.30, 0.52)

    for idx, n in enumerate(stage_ns):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 35.0 >= deadline:
            break
        stage_time = (budget_s - 30.0) * fracs[min(idx, len(fracs) - 1)]
        stage_deadline = min(deadline - 7.0, now + max(25.0, stage_time))
        print("Stage n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.10f" %
              (n, stage_time, best_sc), flush=True)
        a_new, sc_new = run_stage(int(n), stage_deadline, best_a, rng)
        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_a = a_new
            best_sc = sc_new
            print("After stage n=%d best=%.10f" % (n, best_sc), flush=True)
        else:
            print("After stage n=%d unchanged best=%.10f" % (n, best_sc), flush=True)

    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = make_fallback(prev)
        best_sc = score_fft_norm(best_a)[0]

    # Final polish at the incumbent length.
    if time.monotonic() + 25.0 < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
        if rem > 25.0:
            a_opt, sc_opt = local_adam(best_a, rem, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc:
                best_a = a_opt
                best_sc = sc_opt
            print("Final polish best=%.10f" % best_sc, flush=True)

    # Try one larger interpolation only if there is generous time left.
    if time.monotonic() + 150.0 < deadline:
        cur_n = int(best_a.size)
        trial_n = min(8192, 2 * max(512, cur_n))
        if trial_n > cur_n:
            trial_a = interp_seq(best_a, trial_n)
            trial_sc = score_fft_norm(trial_a)[0]
            if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < best_sc + 0.05:
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
                if rem > 40.0:
                    a_opt, sc_opt = local_adam(trial_a, rem, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
                    if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc:
                        best_a = a_opt
                        best_sc = sc_opt
                        print("Larger trial n=%d best=%.10f" % (trial_n, best_sc), flush=True)

    best_a = proj_nonneg_sum(best_a)

    # Validate with the provided exact evaluator when available and reasonably sized.
    if best_a.size <= 20000:
        try:
            exact_sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a]))
            print("FFT score=%.10f exact score=%.10f n=%d" %
                  (best_sc, exact_sc, int(best_a.size)), flush=True)
            if not np.isfinite(exact_sc):
                fb = make_fallback(prev)
                best_a = fb
                best_sc = score_fft_norm(fb)[0]
                print("Exact evaluator invalid; fallback n=%d score=%.10f" %
                      (int(best_a.size), best_sc), flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Exact validation skipped: %s" % str(e), flush=True)
    else:
        print("FFT score=%.10f n=%d (exact omitted for speed)" %
              (best_sc, int(best_a.size)), flush=True)

    out = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in best_a]
    return out
```
